   229	/** Absolute path of the optional detailed-spec file. Single source of truth. */
   230	export function leaderTasklistPath(hopperDir) {
   231	  return join(hopperDir, 'handoffs', 'leader-tasklist.md');
   232	}
   233	
   234	/** Escape a literal string for embedding in a RegExp source. */
   235	function escapeRegExp(s) {
   236	  return s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
   237	}
   238	
   239	/**
   240	 * Build the alternation of the three section-marker forms hopper recognizes, for
   241	 * one escaped id or an already-alternated group of escaped ids (`a|b|c`):
   242	 * `**<idsPattern>**`, `^##+\s+<idsPattern>\b`, `^|\s*<idsPattern>\s*|`. Shared by
   243	 * section-START detection (a single task id) and section-END/boundary detection
   244	 * (root cause (b) fix below — the END search used to only recognize the heading
   245	 * form, so a following task written as `**T-2** …` or `| T-2 | … |` was never a
   246	 * boundary at all and got swallowed into the current section).
   247	 * @param {string} idsPattern  one escaped id, or `id1|id2|...` (already escaped)
   248	 */
   249	function markerAlternation(idsPattern) {
   250	  return `\\*\\*(?:${idsPattern})\\*\\*|^##+\\s+(?:${idsPattern})\\b|^\\|\\s*(?:${idsPattern})\\s*\\|`;
   251	}
   252	
   253	/**
   254	 * Load the DETAILED spec section for a task from .hopper/handoffs/leader-tasklist.md.
   255	 *
   256	 * Returns the section text, or `null` when there is no detailed spec — either
   257	 * because the file has no section for this task-id, or because the file does not
   258	 * exist at all. It NEVER returns prose describing its own failure: this function
   259	 * used to return placeholder strings like
   260	 *   "(no detailed spec found for T-1 in leader-tasklist.md; using queue.md brief only)"
   261	 * which the caller then handed to composePrompt AS the task spec — so the vendor
   262	 * received a handoff whose entire "Task spec" section was a sentence about a
   263	 * missing file, the queue.md Brief was never composed in at all, and the sentence
   264	 * itself was false ("using queue.md brief only" while using nothing). Missing data
   265	 * is now reported as absence (`null`); the caller decides the fallback.
   266	 *
   267	 * Any non-ENOENT I/O error still throws — an unreadable/permission-denied
   268	 * leader-tasklist.md is a real fault and must not be laundered into "no spec".
   269	 *
   270	 * CROSS-TASK LEAK FIX (2026-08-12): section-END detection used to be broken two
   271	 * independent ways, so a task's "spec" could contain ANOTHER task's content —
   272	 * worse than a missing spec, since the vendor executes someone else's task:
   273	 *   (a) `rest.slice(50)` skipped a fixed 50 characters before searching for the
   274	 *       next boundary. A section shorter than 50 chars let the NEXT task's
   275	 *       heading fall inside that skipped window, so it was never seen and the
   276	 *       slice ran on into it. Fixed by searching from right after the matched
   277	 *       marker TEXT instead of a fixed offset — nothing is ever skipped.
   278	 *   (b) the END search only recognized a `^##\s+` heading, while the START
   279	 *       search (and therefore what actually marks a new task) recognizes THREE
   280	 *       forms (bold / heading / table-row). A following task written in bold or
   281	 *       table-row form was never a boundary at all, regardless of section
   282	 *       length. Fixed via `options.otherTaskIds` (see below) — the section now
   283	 *       ends at the next marker naming any OTHER known task id, in any of the
   284	 *       three forms.
   285	 *
   286	 * FOLLOW-UP FIX (same day, adversarial review of the first pass): the boundary
   287	 * is the UNION of two independent checks, not an either/or choice between them —
   288	 * an earlier version made them mutually exclusive (id-aware search REPLACED the
   289	 * heading search whenever `otherTaskIds` was supplied) and that regressed the
   290	 * real dispatch path, which always supplies `otherTaskIds`:
   291	 *   - a plain H2 heading (`^##\s+`, EXACTLY two hashes) always ends a section,
   292	 *     in both modes. This is what lets a task that exists in
   293	 *     leader-tasklist.md but has no queue.md row (e.g. anything dispatched via
   294	 *     `--adhoc`, so it never appears in `otherTaskIds`) still correctly
   295	 *     terminate the PREVIOUS task's section — the union-not-replacement bug
   296	 *     otherwise leaked such a task's content into its predecessor's "spec".
   297	 *   - a bold / any-level-heading / table-row marker naming a KNOWN OTHER task
   298	 *     id (from `otherTaskIds`) ALSO ends a section — this is what recognizes a
   299	 *     following task written in bold or table-row form (root cause (b) above).
   300	 *   The two checks run independently and the EARLIEST match wins; known ids
   301	 *   only ever ADD boundaries beyond the plain-H2 check, they never remove it.
   302	 *   Deliberately H2-ONLY (not `^##+\s+`, any level) for the unconditional
   303	 *   heading check: a spec body legitimately contains its OWN `###`/`####`
   304	 *   subsections (e.g. `### 背景` / `### 验收`), and those must survive intact —
   305	 *   widening to `##+` would cut a spec off at its first subsection.
   306	 *
   307	 * @param {string} hopperDir
   308	 * @param {string} taskId
   309	 * @param {{ otherTaskIds?: string[] }} [options]
   310	 *   `otherTaskIds` — every other known task id (e.g. every id in queue.md).
   311	 *   Exact ids beat pattern-guessing: when supplied, a bold / heading /
   312	 *   table-row marker naming one of them ALSO ends the section (in addition to
   313	 *   the unconditional H2-heading check below) — a legitimate `**Bold**` line
   314	 *   or markdown table inside THIS task's own body can never be mistaken for a
   315	 *   boundary, because it does not spell another KNOWN task's id. Omitted /
   316	 *   empty (e.g. a direct/test caller with no queue.md id list to hand) simply
   317	 *   means this half of the union contributes no extra boundaries — the plain
   318	 *   H2-heading check below still applies either way.
   319	 * @returns {Promise<string|null>}  section text, or null when there is none
   320	 */
   321	export async function loadTaskSpec(hopperDir, taskId, options = {}) {
   322	  const path = leaderTasklistPath(hopperDir);
   323	  let content;
   324	  try {
   325	    content = await readFile(path, 'utf-8');
   326	  } catch (err) {
   327	    if (err.code === 'ENOENT') return null;
   328	    throw err;
   329	  }
   330	  // Find a section starting with **<task-id>** or ## <task-id> or ### <task-id>
   331	  const escapedId = escapeRegExp(taskId);
   332	  const markerRe = new RegExp(markerAlternation(escapedId), 'm');
   333	  const markerMatch = content.match(markerRe);
   334	  if (!markerMatch) return null;
   335	  const sectionStart = markerMatch.index;
   336	  const markerText = markerMatch[0];
   337	  const rest = content.slice(sectionStart);
   338	  // Boundary search space: everything AFTER the matched marker text itself — see
   339	  // root cause (a) in the doc comment above. No fixed offset is skipped.
   340	  const searchSpace = rest.slice(markerText.length);
   341	
   342	  const otherIds = Array.isArray(options.otherTaskIds)
   343	    ? [...new Set(options.otherTaskIds.filter((id) => typeof id === 'string' && id.length > 0 && id !== taskId))]
   344	    : [];
   345	
   346	  // Boundary = the EARLIEST of two independent checks (union, not either/or —
   347	  // see the doc comment's "FOLLOW-UP FIX"):
   348	  let boundaryOffset = -1; // offset into `searchSpace`, or -1 when no boundary found
   349	
   350	  // (i) a plain H2 heading — EXACTLY two hashes — always ends a section, in
   351	  //     both modes. This is what lets an adhoc task with no queue.md row (so
   352	  //     it's absent from otherTaskIds) still terminate the PREVIOUS task's
   353	  //     section via its own heading. H2-only (not `^##+\s+`) so a spec's own
   354	  //     `###`/`####` subsections are never mistaken for a boundary.
   355	  const headingOffset = searchSpace.search(/^##\s+/m);
   356	  if (headingOffset !== -1) boundaryOffset = headingOffset;
   357	
   358	  // (ii) a bold / any-level-heading / table-row marker naming a KNOWN OTHER
   359	  //      task id ALSO ends a section — this recognizes a following task
   360	  //      written in bold or table-row form (root cause (b)). Only ADDS
   361	  //      boundaries beyond (i); never removes the plain-H2 check above.
   362	  if (otherIds.length > 0) {
   363	    const idsPattern = otherIds.map(escapeRegExp).join('|');
   364	    const boundaryRe = new RegExp(markerAlternation(idsPattern), 'm');
   365	    const idOffset = searchSpace.search(boundaryRe);
   366	    if (idOffset !== -1 && (boundaryOffset === -1 || idOffset < boundaryOffset)) {
   367	      boundaryOffset = idOffset;
   368	    }
   369	  }
   370	  const end = boundaryOffset === -1 ? Math.min(rest.length, 8000) : markerText.length + boundaryOffset;
   371	  const section = rest.slice(0, end).trim();
   372	  // Fail-closed rule: decide on "is there actual BODY after the matched marker",
   373	  // not "is the section non-empty" — the section always includes the marker line
   374	  // itself, so a bare "## T-1" heading with nothing under it previously satisfied
   375	  // `section.length > 0` and returned a non-null "spec" that was just the heading.
   376	  // That let the marker slip past composeTaskContent's fail-closed throw entirely
   377	  // (a matched-but-bodyless section looked identical to a real spec). Strip the
   378	  // matched marker text itself off the front — `rest` starts exactly at the
   379	  // match, so `section` does too — and require non-whitespace remainder.
   380	  const afterMarker = section.startsWith(markerText) ? section.slice(markerText.length) : section;
   381	  return afterMarker.trim().length > 0 ? section : null;
   382	}
   383	
   384	/** Header that labels the queue.md Brief inside a merged task spec. */
   385	export const QUEUE_BRIEF_HEADING = '### Queue brief';
   386	
   387	/**
   388	 * Precedence line appended to a MERGED spec. The vendor sees two sources of task
   389	 * content; it must be told which wins, or a stale Brief cell can silently
   390	 * out-argue the detailed spec it was supposed to summarize.
   391	 */
   392	export const QUEUE_BRIEF_PRECEDENCE_NOTE =
   393	  '(Source: the Brief column of .hopper/queue.md. Where it conflicts with the detailed spec above, the detailed spec wins.)';
   394	
   395	/**
   396	 * Assemble the task CONTENT for a queued dispatch out of its two possible
   397	 * sources, and say (to the operator, never to the vendor) when one is missing.
   398	 *
   399	 * Rules:
   400	 *   - both present  → detailed spec first, then the Brief under `### Queue brief`,
   401	 *                     with an explicit "detailed spec wins on conflict" line
   402	 *   - spec only     → the spec verbatim
   403	 *   - brief only    → the brief verbatim (mirrors the ad-hoc path, where the brief
   404	 *                     IS the spec) + an operator notice explaining why there is no
   405	 *                     detailed section
   406	 *   - neither       → THROW (fail closed). Dispatching a content-free prompt is how
   407	 *                     a vendor "succeeds" (exit 0, status done) at nothing. Matches
   408	 *                     the ad-hoc/swarm paths, which already reject an empty brief.
   409	 *
   410	 * Note `??` is unusable here: queue.js gives a missing Brief cell the EMPTY STRING,
   411	 * and `'' ?? brief` is `''` — nullish coalescing only catches null/undefined.
   412	 * Everything below is emptiness-aware via .trim().
   413	 *
   414	 * @param {{hopperDir: string, taskId: string, task: {brief?: string}, otherTaskIds?: string[]}} args
   415	 *   `otherTaskIds` — passed straight through to loadTaskSpec's exact-boundary
   416	 *   resolution (every other known task id from queue.md); see its doc comment.
   417	 * @returns {Promise<{taskSpec: string, specNotice: string|null}>}
   418	 */
   419	async function composeTaskContent({ hopperDir, taskId, task, otherTaskIds }) {
   420	  const detailed = (await loadTaskSpec(hopperDir, taskId, { otherTaskIds }) || '').trim();
   421	  const brief = typeof task?.brief === 'string' ? task.brief.trim() : '';
   422	
   423	  if (detailed && brief) {
   424	    return {
   425	      taskSpec: `${detailed}\n\n${QUEUE_BRIEF_HEADING}\n\n${brief}\n\n${QUEUE_BRIEF_PRECEDENCE_NOTE}`,
   426	      specNotice: null,
   427	    };
   428	  }
   429	  if (detailed) return { taskSpec: detailed, specNotice: null };
   430	
   431	  // No detailed section. Distinguish "file has no entry for this id" from "no file
   432	  // at all" — same two situations the old placeholder strings described, except the
   433	  // text now goes to the OPERATOR (stderr / --resolve echo) instead of being fed to
   434	  // the vendor as if it were the task.
   435	  const path = leaderTasklistPath(hopperDir);
   436	  const tasklistExists = await fileExists(path);
   437	  if (brief) {
   438	    return {
   439	      taskSpec: brief,
   440	      specNotice: tasklistExists
   441	        ? `No detailed spec section for ${taskId} in leader-tasklist.md; task content comes from queue.md Brief.`
   442	        : `leader-tasklist.md is absent at ${path}; task content comes from queue.md Brief.`,
   443	    };
   444	  }
   445	  throw new Error(
   446	    `Task ${taskId} has no task content: queue.md Brief is empty and no detailed spec was found`
   447	    + (tasklistExists ? ` (no section for ${taskId} in ${path})` : ` (${path} does not exist)`)
   448	    + `. Fill the Brief cell for ${taskId} in .hopper/queue.md, or add a "## ${taskId}" section to leader-tasklist.md.`
   449	  );
   450	}
   451	
   452	async function fileExists(path) {
   453	  try {
   454	    await access(path);
   455	    return true;
   456	  } catch (err) {
   457	    // Only "does not exist" maps to false. Anything else (EACCES, etc.) is a real
   458	    // fault and must not be misreported as "file does not exist".
   459	    if (err.code === 'ENOENT') return false;
   460	    throw err;
   461	  }
   462	}
   463	
   464	/**
   465	 * Status summary for --status command.
     1	// Cross-task content LEAK fix in loadTaskSpec()'s section-END detection.
     2	// Anchor: tests/unit/dispatch-task-spec-boundary.test.js
     3	//
     4	// Found by an adversarial review of the queue-brief-dropped-without-leader-tasklist
     5	// fix (docs/archive/ISSUES.md) and independently reproduced. loadTaskSpec()'s
     6	// section-END detection was broken TWO INDEPENDENT ways, so a task's "spec" could
     7	// contain ANOTHER task's content — worse than a missing spec, since the vendor
     8	// then executes someone else's task instead of its own:
     9	//
    10	//   (a) `rest.slice(50)` skipped a fixed 50 characters before searching for the
    11	//       next section boundary. A section shorter than 50 chars let the NEXT
    12	//       task's heading fall inside that skipped window, so it was never seen
    13	//       and the slice ran on into (and swallowed) that next task.
    14	//   (b) the END search only ever recognized a `^##\s+` heading, while the START
    15	//       search (what actually MARKS a new task, per the same file) recognizes
    16	//       THREE forms: `**<id>**` (bold), `^##+\s+<id>` (heading), and
    17	//       `^|\s*<id>\s*|` (table row). A following task written in bold or
    18	//       table-row form was therefore never a boundary at all, REGARDLESS of
    19	//       section length.
    20	//
    21	// Fix: loadTaskSpec(hopperDir, taskId, { otherTaskIds }) — resolveDispatch (which
    22	// already parses queue.md and holds every known task id) now passes every OTHER
    23	// known id, and the section ends at the exact next marker (any of the three
    24	// forms) naming one of them. Exact ids beat pattern-guessing: a legitimate
    25	// `**Bold**` line or markdown table inside a task's OWN body can never be
    26	// mistaken for a boundary, because it does not spell another task's id. The
    27	// `rest.slice(50)` magic number is gone entirely — the search now starts right
    28	// after the matched marker TEXT, in both the id-aware and no-id-list fallback
    29	// modes.
    30	//
    31	// FOLLOW-UP (same day, adversarial review of the first pass — two more defects,
    32	// both in the union/either-or shape of the boundary check, not the marker forms
    33	// themselves):
    34	//
    35	//   Defect 1 — the first pass made the boundary an EITHER/OR: id-aware search
    36	//   ONLY when `otherTaskIds` was supplied, heading-only search ONLY when it was
    37	//   not. Since `resolveDispatch` (the real dispatch path) ALWAYS supplies
    38	//   `otherTaskIds`, a plain markdown heading stopped being a boundary at all on
    39	//   that path — any task present in leader-tasklist.md but ABSENT from
    40	//   queue.md (e.g. anything dispatched via `--adhoc`, which never gets a
    41	//   queue.md row) could no longer terminate the PREVIOUS task's section, and
    42	//   its content leaked backwards. Fixed: the boundary is now the UNION of (i)
    43	//   an unconditional H2-heading check and (ii) the known-other-id check —
    44	//   whichever comes first — so known ids only ever ADD boundaries, never
    45	//   remove the heading one.
    46	//
    47	//   Defect 2 — the heading half of that union (and the original no-id
    48	//   fallback) used `^##+\s+` (two OR MORE hashes), so a spec body's own
    49	//   legitimate `###`/`####` subsections (e.g. `### 背景` / `### 验收`) were
    50	//   mistaken for a boundary and cut the spec off at the first one. Fixed:
    51	//   narrowed to `^##\s+` — EXACTLY two hashes — matching what the pre-round
    52	//   code used for its (correct, if magic-numbered) H2 check.
    53	
    54	import { test } from 'node:test';
    55	import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
    56	import { resolveDispatch, loadTaskSpec } from '../../cli/src/dispatch.js';
    57	import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
    58	import { join } from 'node:path';
    59	import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
    60	
    61	/**
    62	 * @param {object} opts
    63	 * @param {string} opts.tasklist     leader-tasklist.md contents
    64	 * @param {string} [opts.brief1]     T-1's queue.md Brief cell
    65	 * @param {string} [opts.brief2]     T-2's queue.md Brief cell
    66	 */
    67	function scaffold({ tasklist, brief1 = 'do T-1 things', brief2 = 'do T-2 things' }) {
    68	  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'hopper-boundary-'));
    69	  const hopperDir = join(root, '.hopper');
    70	  mkdirSync(join(hopperDir, 'tasks'), { recursive: true });
    71	  mkdirSync(join(hopperDir, 'handoffs'), { recursive: true });
    72	  writeFileSync(join(hopperDir, 'queue.md'), [
    73	    '## Tasks',
    74	    '',
    75	    '| ID | Task-type | Status | Brief |',
    76	    '|----|-----------|--------|-------|',
    77	    `| T-1 | code-impl | pending | ${brief1} |`,
    78	    `| T-2 | code-impl | pending | ${brief2} |`,
    79	    '',
    80	  ].join('\n'));
    81	  writeFileSync(join(hopperDir, 'tasks', 'code-impl.md'), '# Frame\nImplement.');
    82	  writeFileSync(join(hopperDir, 'handoffs', 'leader-tasklist.md'), tasklist);
    83	  writeFileSync(join(hopperDir, 'AGENTS.md'), [
    84	    '## Approved Vendors',
    85	    '',
    86	    '| Vendor | Approved |',
    87	    '|---|---|',
    88	    '| codex | yes |',
    89	    '',
    90	    '## Task-type → vendor default preference',
    91	    '',
    92	    '| Task-type | Default vendor | Why |',
    93	    '|---|---|---|',
    94	    '| code-impl | codex | x |',
    95	    '',
    96	  ].join('\n'));
    97	  return { root, hopperDir };
    98	}
    99	
   100	// ─── root cause (a): the `rest.slice(50)` magic number ──────────────────────
   101	
   102	test('(a) short section (<50 chars): T-1 does not swallow T-2 (was: leaked)', async () => {
   103	  const { root, hopperDir } = scaffold({
   104	    tasklist: [
   105	      '## T-1',
   106	      '',
   107	      '## T-2',
   108	      'SECRET_T2_BODY_ONLY belongs to T-2 alone.',
   109	      '',
   110	    ].join('\n'),
   111	  });
   112	  try {
   113	    // T-1's own section is just the bare heading — no body of its own. Correctly
   114	    // finding the T-2 boundary means loadTaskSpec reports "no spec" (null), not a
   115	    // leaked chunk of T-2's content pretending to be T-1's.
   116	    const spec1 = await loadTaskSpec(hopperDir, 'T-1');
   117	    assert.equal(spec1, null, 'T-1 has no body of its own once the T-2 boundary is found correctly');
   118	    assert.doesNotMatch(String(spec1), /SECRET_T2_BODY_ONLY/);
   119	    // Control: T-2 itself still sees its own real content.
   120	    const spec2 = await loadTaskSpec(hopperDir, 'T-2');
   121	    assert.match(spec2, /SECRET_T2_BODY_ONLY/);
   122	  } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
   123	});
   124	
   125	test('(a) control: padding T-1 past 50 chars finds the boundary correctly (isolates the magic number as the cause)', async () => {
   126	  const { root, hopperDir } = scaffold({
   127	    tasklist: [
   128	      '## T-1',
   129	      '',
   130	      'Real T-1 body padded well past fifty characters of legitimate content so the',
   131	      'old fixed-offset skip would have found this boundary too.',
   132	      '',

   135	      '',
   136	    ].join('\n'),
   137	  });
   138	  try {
   139	    const spec1 = await loadTaskSpec(hopperDir, 'T-1');
   140	    assert.match(spec1, /Real T-1 body padded/);
   141	    assert.doesNotMatch(spec1, /SECRET_T2_BODY_ONLY/);
   142	  } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }

   147	test('(b) bold-form next task ("**T-2** ...") is a boundary regardless of section length (was: never a boundary)', async () => {

   157	      '',
   158	    ].join('\n'),
   159	  });
   160	  try {
   161	    const spec1 = await loadTaskSpec(hopperDir, 'T-1', { otherTaskIds: ['T-1', 'T-2'] });

   163	    assert.doesNotMatch(spec1, /SECRET_T2_BOLD/, 'T-2 bold-form section must not leak into T-1');
   164	  } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
   165	});
   166	
   167	test('(b) table-row-form next task ("| T-2 | ... |") is a boundary regardless of section length (was: never a boundary)', async () => {

   184	  } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
   185	});

   187	test('(b) control: heading-form next task ("## T-2") was already a correct boundary before this fix', async () => {
   188	  const { root, hopperDir } = scaffold({

   206	
   207	// ─── over-truncation guard (hard constraint) ─────────────────────────────────
   208	// A spec body legitimately containing bold text at the start of a line, or a
   209	// markdown table, must NOT be mistaken for a section boundary — even when an
   210	// id-shaped token (but NOT a real, OTHER, known task id) appears inside it.
   211	
   212	test('over-truncation guard: a bold line — including one naming an id-shaped token that is NOT a known other task — stays in the body', async () => {
   213	  const { root, hopperDir } = scaffold({
   214	    tasklist: [
   215	      '## T-1',
   216	      '',
   217	      '**Bold** statement at the start of a line, part of T-1\'s own legitimate body text.',
   218	      '',
   219	      'It also mentions **T-9** inline, which is NOT a real task id in this queue (only',
   220	      'T-1 and T-2 exist) and must not be treated as a section boundary.',
   221	      '',
   222	      'REST_OF_T1_BODY_MARKER: the rest of T-1\'s body continues here and must survive intact.',
   223	      '',
   224	    ].join('\n'),
   225	  });
   226	  try {
   227	    const spec1 = await loadTaskSpec(hopperDir, 'T-1', { otherTaskIds: ['T-1', 'T-2'] });
   228	    assert.match(spec1, /\*\*Bold\*\* statement/, 'the bold line at the top of the body is not cut');
   229	    assert.match(spec1, /\*\*T-9\*\*/, 'a bold id-shaped token that is not a KNOWN other task stays in the body');
   230	    assert.match(spec1, /REST_OF_T1_BODY_MARKER/, 'content after the bold lines is not truncated');
   231	  } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
   232	});
   233	
   234	test('over-truncation guard: a markdown table inside the body (not naming another known task) stays in the body', async () => {
   235	  const { root, hopperDir } = scaffold({
   236	    tasklist: [
   237	      '## T-1',
   238	      '',
   239	      'Here is a table describing something unrelated to task ids:',
   240	      '',
   241	      '| Name | Value |',
   242	      '|------|-------|',
   243	      '| foo | bar |',
   244	      '| baz | qux |',
   245	      '',
   246	      'BODY_CONTINUES_AFTER_TABLE_MARKER: this line must still be included.',
   247	      '',
   248	    ].join('\n'),
   249	  });
   250	  try {
   251	    const spec1 = await loadTaskSpec(hopperDir, 'T-1', { otherTaskIds: ['T-1', 'T-2'] });
   252	    assert.match(spec1, /\| foo \| bar \|/, 'table rows survive');
   253	    assert.match(spec1, /BODY_CONTINUES_AFTER_TABLE_MARKER/, 'content after the table is not truncated');
   254	  } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
   255	});
   256	
   257	// ─── full pipeline (resolveDispatch): the wiring, not just the unit ─────────
   258	
   259	test('resolveDispatch: T-1 never receives T-2\'s bold-form content in its composed prompt (end-to-end)', async () => {
   260	  const { root, hopperDir } = scaffold({
   261	    tasklist: [
   262	      '## T-1',
   263	      '',
   264	      'Real T-1 body that is intentionally long enough to rule out the magic-number',
   265	      'cause entirely — well past fifty characters describing what T-1 needs to do.',
   266	      '',
   267	      '**T-2** SECRET_T2_BOLD_E2E belongs to T-2 alone.',
   268	      '',
   269	    ].join('\n'),
   270	  });
   271	  try {
   272	    const r1 = await resolveDispatch({ hopperDir, taskId: 'T-1' });
   273	    assert.match(r1.taskSpec, /Real T-1 body that is intentionally long/);
   274	    assert.doesNotMatch(r1.taskSpec, /SECRET_T2_BOLD_E2E/, 'taskSpec must not leak T-2 content');
   275	    assert.doesNotMatch(r1.composedPrompt, /SECRET_T2_BOLD_E2E/, 'composed vendor prompt must not leak T-2 content');
   276	  } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
   277	});
   278	
   279	test('resolveDispatch: T-2 (last section, no trailing boundary) still gets its own full content', async () => {
   280	  const { root, hopperDir } = scaffold({
   281	    tasklist: [
   282	      '## T-1',
   283	      '',
   284	      'Real T-1 body that is intentionally long enough to rule out the magic-number',
   285	      'cause entirely — well past fifty characters describing what T-1 needs to do.',
   286	      '',
   287	      '**T-2** SECRET_T2_BOLD_E2E belongs to T-2 alone.',
   288	      '',
   289	    ].join('\n'),
   290	  });

   293	    assert.match(r2.taskSpec, /SECRET_T2_BOLD_E2E/);
   294	  } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
   295	});
   296	
   297	// ─── re-verifies docs/archive/ISSUES.md's fail-closed claim ─────────────────
   298	// The Resolution section of queue-brief-dropped-without-leader-tasklist claims
   299	// "spec 与 brief 皆空 → 抛错（fail-closed）". That claim was FALSIFIED by this very
   300	// leak: a task with NO real content of its own (a bodyless "## T-1" heading) and
   301	// an empty Brief should fail-closed — but under the broken boundary detection,
   302	// a following task in bold/table form was never seen as a boundary, so
   303	// loadTaskSpec silently returned that OTHER task's leaked content as if it were
   304	// T-1's own non-empty spec, and the fail-closed throw never fired. With both
   305	// root causes fixed, this must throw again.
   306	test('fail-closed re-verified: empty Brief + bodyless T-1 immediately followed by a bold-form T-2 still throws (was: leaked content silently defeated the fail-closed guard)', async () => {
   307	  const { root, hopperDir } = scaffold({
   308	    brief1: '',
   309	    tasklist: [
   310	      '## T-1',
   311	      '',
   312	      '**T-2** SECRET_T2_BOLD real content that belongs to T-2 alone.',
   313	      '',
   314	    ].join('\n'),
   315	  });
   316	  try {
   317	    const spec1 = await loadTaskSpec(hopperDir, 'T-1', { otherTaskIds: ['T-1', 'T-2'] });
   318	    assert.equal(spec1, null, 'T-1 has no body of its own once the bold-form T-2 boundary is found correctly');
   319	    await assert.rejects(
   320	      () => resolveDispatch({ hopperDir, taskId: 'T-1' }),
   321	      /Task T-1 has no task content: queue\.md Brief is empty and no detailed spec was found/,
   322	      'fail-closed must fire — not silently substitute T-2\'s leaked content',
   323	    );
   324	  } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
   325	});
   326	
   327	// ─── union defect 1: a plain H2 heading must ALWAYS terminate a section, even ──
   328	// ─── for an id that is NOT in otherTaskIds (e.g. an --adhoc task with no ────────
   329	// ─── queue.md row) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   330	// Reproduced by the coordinator: with otherTaskIds supplied (the real dispatch
   331	// path, which ALWAYS supplies it), an earlier version of the fix made the
   332	// id-aware search REPLACE the heading search instead of adding to it — so a
   333	// following task written as a plain "## T-91" heading, when T-91 has no
   334	// queue.md row (and is therefore absent from otherTaskIds), was no longer a
   335	// boundary AT ALL, and its content leaked into the previous task's spec.
   336	
   337	test('union defect 1: T-1 spec called with otherTaskIds does NOT leak into a following "## T-91" whose id is absent from otherTaskIds', async () => {
   338	  const { root, hopperDir } = scaffold({
   339	    tasklist: [
   340	      '## T-1',
   341	      '',
   342	      'Real T-1 body that is intentionally long enough to rule out the magic-number',
   343	      'cause entirely — well past fifty characters describing what T-1 actually',
   344	      'needs to do, on its own, before any other task marker appears below.',
   345	      '',
   346	      '## T-91',
   347	      'SECRET_ADHOC belongs to T-91, which was dispatched via --adhoc and has no queue.md row.',
   348	      '',
   349	    ].join('\n'),
   350	  });
   351	  try {
   352	    // otherTaskIds mirrors the real queue.md id set — T-91 is NOT in it, exactly
   353	    // like an --adhoc task (T-091..T-100 in the real repo) that never gets a
   354	    // queue.md row.
   355	    const spec1 = await loadTaskSpec(hopperDir, 'T-1', { otherTaskIds: ['T-1', 'T-2'] });
   356	    assert.match(spec1, /Real T-1 body that is intentionally long/, 'T-1 own body preserved');
   357	    assert.doesNotMatch(spec1, /SECRET_ADHOC/, 'a plain heading must terminate the section even for an UNKNOWN id');
   358	  } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
   359	});
   360	
   361	// ─── union defect 2: the unconditional heading check must be H2-ONLY ────────
   362	// ─── (`^##\s+`, not `^##+\s+`) so a spec's own ###/#### subsections survive ──
   363	// Reproduced by the coordinator: widening the heading check to "any level"
   364	// mistakes a spec's own `### 背景` / `### 验收` subsections for a section
   365	// boundary and truncates the spec at the first one. Checked in BOTH modes —
   366	// with and without otherTaskIds — since the heading check is unconditional.
   367	
   368	function subsectionTasklist() {
   369	  return [
   370	    '## T-1',
   371	    '',
   372	    '开头正文。',
   373	    '',
   374	    '### 背景',
   375	    '… KEEPME_SUB',
   376	    '',
   377	    '### 验收',
   378	    '… KEEPME_END',
   379	    '',
   380	  ].join('\n');
   381	}
   382	
   383	test('union defect 2: a spec\'s own ### subsections survive intact — WITH otherTaskIds (real dispatch path)', async () => {
   384	  const { root, hopperDir } = scaffold({ tasklist: subsectionTasklist() });
   385	  try {
   386	    const spec1 = await loadTaskSpec(hopperDir, 'T-1', { otherTaskIds: ['T-1', 'T-2'] });
   387	    assert.match(spec1, /开头正文/);
   388	    assert.match(spec1, /### 背景/);
   389	    assert.match(spec1, /KEEPME_SUB/);
   390	    assert.match(spec1, /### 验收/);
   391	    assert.match(spec1, /KEEPME_END/);
   392	  } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
   393	});
   394	
   395	test('union defect 2: a spec\'s own ### subsections survive intact — WITHOUT otherTaskIds (no-id-list mode)', async () => {
   396	  const { root, hopperDir } = scaffold({ tasklist: subsectionTasklist() });
   397	  try {
   398	    const spec1 = await loadTaskSpec(hopperDir, 'T-1');
   399	    assert.match(spec1, /开头正文/);
   400	    assert.match(spec1, /### 背景/);
   401	    assert.match(spec1, /KEEPME_SUB/);
   402	    assert.match(spec1, /### 验收/);
   403	    assert.match(spec1, /KEEPME_END/);
   404	  } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
   405	});
   406	
   407	test('union defect 2 (end-to-end): resolveDispatch keeps a spec\'s own ### subsections intact in the composed prompt', async () => {
   408	  const { root, hopperDir } = scaffold({ tasklist: subsectionTasklist() });
   409	  try {
   410	    const r1 = await resolveDispatch({ hopperDir, taskId: 'T-1' });
   411	    assert.match(r1.taskSpec, /KEEPME_SUB/);
   412	    assert.match(r1.taskSpec, /KEEPME_END/);
   413	    assert.match(r1.composedPrompt, /KEEPME_SUB/);
   414	    assert.match(r1.composedPrompt, /KEEPME_END/);
   415	  } finally { rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
   416	});

<a id="task-spec-structural-only-body-accepted"></a>

## task-spec-structural-only-body-accepted

---

## ISSUE：leader-tasklist 小节的"正文"只有结构性标记时仍被当作有效 spec

- **版本**：hopper 0.55.0（由 T-102 复审的 grok 一路实跑发现；登记前已用真实 `loadTaskSpec` 复核，见下）
- **严重度**：低——需要人写出这种 leader-tasklist 才会触发，但**失败形状与已修的两处完全相同**
- **状态**：**Open — 未修，仅登记**

### 现象

`loadTaskSpec()` 的 fail-closed 判据是「匹配 marker 之后有无非空白正文」
（`cli/src/dispatch.js:380-381`：`afterMarker.trim().length > 0 ? section : null`）。
一个正文只有结构性标记（如 `---` 分隔线、空表格骨架 `|---|---|`、单个 `>` 引用符）的小节，
**非空白，因此被判为有效 spec 并派发**——vendor 拿到一份没有任务内容的任务书。

### 实测复核（2026-08-12，登记前用真实代码验证，非转述）

直接调用 `loadTaskSpec` 对四个 fixture 小节验证（`otherTaskIds` 传全部四个 id 以确保边界正确）：

| 小节正文 | `loadTaskSpec` 返回值 |
|---|---|
| `---` | 非 null —— `"## T-1\n\n---"` |
| `\|---\|---\|` | 非 null —— `"## T-2\n\n\|---\|---\|"` |
| `>` | 非 null —— `"## T-3\n\n>"` |
| `Real content line.`（对照） | 非 null —— `"## T-4\n\nReal content line."` |

三个「仅结构性标记」的正文全部被接受为有效 spec，返回值形状与「真有内容」的对照组完全一致——
证实了本条问题的判断，不是猜测。

### 与已修两处的关系

这是**同一族的第三个实例**：

| # | 实例 | 状态 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 无 leader-tasklist 条目时返回自述文案冒充 spec | 已修 0.55.0 |
| 2 | 裸 marker（`## T-1` 光标题没正文）非空 → 冒充 spec | 已修 0.55.0 |
| 3 | 正文只有结构性标记 → 冒充 spec | **本条，未修** |

共同形状：**「看起来有内容」与「真的承载了任务」被当成了同一件事。**

### 建议的修法

判据从「有非空白字符」收紧为「去掉纯结构性标记后仍有实质内容」。
需要注意不要过度收紧——正文里合法包含表格或分隔线的 spec 必须仍被接受，
判据应是「**除了**结构性标记之外还有别的」，不是「不含结构性标记」。

---

<a id="composeprompt-no-fail-closed-on-empty-spec"></a>

## composeprompt-no-fail-closed-on-empty-spec

---

## ISSUE：`composePrompt` 对空/纯空白 taskSpec 没有 fail-closed，只靠上游拦截

- **版本**：hopper 0.55.0（T-102 复审的 grok 提出，`cli/src/tasks.js:169`；登记前已核对行号与实测输出，见下）
- **严重度**：低——当前 `dispatch.js` 已在上游拦住，属纵深防御缺失而非活跃缺陷
- **状态**：**Open — 未修，仅登记**

### 现象

`composePrompt(frameContent, taskSpec, …)` 不校验 `taskSpec` 是否为空或纯空白，
直接拼进「## Task spec」一节（`cli/src/tasks.js:169`：
`` parts.push(`## Task spec\n\n${taskSpec.trim()}`); ``）。**当前唯一的防线在 `dispatch.js` 的
`composeTaskContent()`**，即任何绕过该函数直接调用 `composePrompt` 的新调用点，都会重新打开
0.55.0 刚堵上的洞。

### 实测复核（2026-08-12，登记前用真实代码验证，非转述）

直接调用 `composePrompt('# Frame', '')` 与 `composePrompt('# Frame', '   \n  ')`：两次调用都
**未抛错**，都正常返回一份完整 prompt，其中「## Task spec」小节之后没有任何内容
（`...## Task spec\n\n\n`）。确认没有任何 empty/whitespace 守卫。

### 为什么本轮没修

`cli/src/tasks.js` 被 `tests/unit/tasks.test.js` 的 **4 条逐字节 `assert.equal`**
（`:130`/`:138`/`:150`/`:155`）锁死拼装形状，本轮修复的既定约束是**一字不改该文件**。

注：在函数入口加 fail-closed 抛错**不会**改变合法输入的拼装结果，
因此理论上与那 4 条断言不冲突——是本轮的 scope 约束，不是技术阻碍。留待独立一轮评估。

// Integration tests against REAL .hopper/ fixtures (codex Phase 1 audit F4 fix)
// Anchor: tests/integration/real-fixtures.test.js
//
// Synthetic unit tests cover happy path; this file verifies the dispatch
// chain works against the actual files committed in this repo.

import { test } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { resolveDispatch, getStatus } from '../../cli/src/dispatch.js';
import { parseAgentsFile, resolveVendor } from '../../cli/src/agents.js';
import { loadTaskFrame, verifyFrameAntiPersona, listTaskTypes } from '../../cli/src/tasks.js';
import { parseQueue, findEligibleTask } from '../../cli/src/queue.js';
import { SCAFFOLD_TASK_TYPES } from '../../cli/src/scaffold.js';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { dirname, resolve, join } from 'node:path';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = dirname(__filename);
const REPO_ROOT = resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');
const HOPPER_DIR = join(REPO_ROOT, '.hopper');

test('integration: parseQueue reads real queue.md without errors', async () => {
  const tasks = await parseQueue(join(HOPPER_DIR, 'queue.md'));
  // Real queue has 19 tasks per spec v2.0.3
  assert.ok(tasks.length >= 18, `expected ≥18 tasks, got ${tasks.length}`);
  // Specific known tasks should be present
  const ids = tasks.map((t) => t.id);
  assert.ok(ids.includes('T-PLUGIN-00'));
  assert.ok(ids.includes('T-PLUGIN-05e'));
  assert.ok(ids.includes('T-PLUGIN-10'));
});

test('integration: real queue.md uses v2 Task-type schema (not v1 Role)', async () => {
  const tasks = await parseQueue(join(HOPPER_DIR, 'queue.md'));
  // Every task should have a recognized task-type
  const validTypes = ['spec-write', 'code-impl', 'code-review-adversarial',
    'code-review-acceptance', 'sidecar-polish', 'spec-blindspot-hunt'];
  for (const t of tasks) {
    assert.ok(validTypes.includes(t.taskType),
      `task ${t.id} has unknown task-type '${t.taskType}'`);
  }
});

test('integration: all real task-type frames pass anti-persona check', async () => {
  const types = await listTaskTypes(HOPPER_DIR);
  // Derived from the shipped registry, not a hardcoded count. The literal `6` here
  // turned every task-type addition into a CI failure discovered only after push —
  // and `npm test` globs tests/unit/ only, so it could not be seen locally either.
  // What this test is actually for is the anti-persona check below; the count is a
  // guard that the fixture workspace has not silently lost its frames.
  assert.equal(types.length, SCAFFOLD_TASK_TYPES.length,
    `this repo's own .hopper/tasks/ should hold one frame per shipped task-type; `
    + `got ${types.length} of ${SCAFFOLD_TASK_TYPES.length}. `
    + 'Run `hopper-dispatch --migrate-config --yes` to add the missing ones.');

  for (const type of types) {
    const frame = await loadTaskFrame(HOPPER_DIR, type);
    const result = verifyFrameAntiPersona(frame);
    assert.ok(result.ok,
      `Frame '${type}.md' has persona violations: ${JSON.stringify(result.hits)}`);
  }
});

test('integration: parseAgentsFile reads real AGENTS.md and produces normalized vendor IDs', async () => {
  const { agents, preferences } = await parseAgentsFile(join(HOPPER_DIR, 'AGENTS.md'));
  assert.ok(agents.length >= 5, `expected ≥5 agents, got ${agents.length}`);

  // Per codex Phase 1 F2 fix: vendor IDs must match cli/src/vendors/<vendor>.js convention
  // (i.e. NOT 'codex-cli' / 'kimi-cli' / 'agy-cli' but normalized 'codex' / 'kimi' / 'agy')
  const vendors = agents.map((a) => a.vendor);
  const expectedNormalizedVendors = ['codex', 'kimi', 'opencode', 'copilot', 'agy'];
  for (const expected of expectedNormalizedVendors) {
    assert.ok(
      vendors.includes(expected) || vendors.includes('claude-code-tui'),
      `expected normalized vendor '${expected}' in agents list; got: ${JSON.stringify(vendors)}`,
    );
  }
  // Specifically: NO vendor should still have '-cli' suffix
  for (const v of vendors) {
    assert.ok(!v.endsWith('-cli'), `vendor '${v}' still has -cli suffix; normalization failed`);
  }
});

test('integration: real AGENTS.md task-type preferences resolve to known vendors', async () => {
  const { agents, preferences } = await parseAgentsFile(join(HOPPER_DIR, 'AGENTS.md'));
  // Each preference should point at a nickname that exists in agents list
  const nicknames = new Set(agents.map((a) => a.nickname));
  for (const [taskType, vendorPref] of Object.entries(preferences)) {
    assert.ok(
      nicknames.has(vendorPref) || agents.some((a) => a.vendor === vendorPref),
      `preference for '${taskType}' points at '${vendorPref}' but no matching nickname/vendor found`,
    );
  }
});

test('integration: resolveDispatch end-to-end for a known-eligible task scenario', async () => {
  // Find a task whose deps are all done (or no deps) AND status is pending
  const tasks = await parseQueue(join(HOPPER_DIR, 'queue.md'));
  const eligibleTask = tasks.find((t) => {
    if (t.status !== 'pending') return false;
    if (t.depends.length === 0) return true;
    return t.depends.every((depId) => tasks.find((d) => d.id === depId)?.status === 'done');
  });

  if (!eligibleTask) {
    // No eligible task right now — verify dispatch handles "ineligible" cleanly
    // by picking a known-ineligible task and asserting error
    const known = tasks.find((t) => t.status !== 'pending');
    if (known) {
      await assert.rejects(
        () => resolveDispatch({ hopperDir: HOPPER_DIR, taskId: known.id }),
        /Task not eligible/,
      );
    }
    return;
  }

  // Eligible task found — resolve should succeed
  const result = await resolveDispatch({ hopperDir: HOPPER_DIR, taskId: eligibleTask.id });
  assert.ok(result.task);
  assert.equal(result.task.id, eligibleTask.id);
  assert.ok(result.vendor);
  assert.ok(result.composedPrompt.length > 0);
  assert.ok(result.frame.length > 0);
});

test('integration: getStatus returns valid summary against real queue.md', async () => {
  const summary = await getStatus(HOPPER_DIR);
  assert.ok(summary.total >= 18);
  assert.equal(typeof summary.pending, 'number');
  assert.equal(typeof summary.done, 'number');
  // Sum of statuses must equal total (or close — some defensive '|| 0' bookkeeping)
  const sum = summary.pending + summary['in-progress'] + summary.done + summary.failed + summary.removed;
  assert.equal(sum, summary.total);
});

# Issue archive

Field reports and defect records produced while building Hopper — one file per issue,
consolidated here on 2026-08-05 (previously 18 loose `ISSUE-*.md` files in the repository
root).

**Why consolidate.** Scattered across the root they answered no question well: you had to
open all eighteen to learn which were still open, and they crowded out the files a reader
actually needs first (README, MIGRATION, CHANGELOG). Here the status index below answers
that in one glance, which makes the OPEN ones *more* visible than they were, not less.

**Why keep them at all.** Several are cited from source comments as the reason a piece of
code is shaped the way it is; deleting them would strip that rationale. They are evidence,
so the bodies are reproduced **verbatim** — including their original mix of English and
Chinese. Historical records are not rewritten here; only this framing is new.

---

## Status index

### Open — 9

 # Issue archive
 
 Field reports and defect records produced while building Hopper — one file per issue,
 consolidated here on 2026-08-05 (previously 18 loose `ISSUE-*.md` files in the repository
 root).
 
 **Why consolidate.** Scattered across the root they answered no question well: you had to
 open all eighteen to learn which were still open, and they crowded out the files a reader
 actually needs first (README, MIGRATION, CHANGELOG). Here the status index below answers
 that in one glance, which makes the OPEN ones *more* visible than they were, not less.
 
 **Why keep them at all.** Several are cited from source comments as the reason a piece of
 code is shaped the way it is; deleting them would strip that rationale. They are evidence,
 so the bodies are reproduced **verbatim** — including their original mix of English and
 Chinese. Historical records are not rewritten here; only this framing is new.

 <a id="grok-models-succeeds-but-hopper-dispatch-auth-failed"></a>
 
 ## grok-models-succeeds-but-hopper-dispatch-auth-failed
 
 ---
 
 ## ISSUE: `grok models` 成功，但 `hopper-dispatch` 真实派发终止为 `adapter-auth-failed`
 
 > 状态：待 Hopper 开发者调查（根因未定）
 >
 > 首次记录：2026-08-05
 > 严重性：高——已完成 OAuth 登录的同一 Grok CLI 可以取得 live model catalog，
 > 但 Hopper 的真实 read-only 派发在执行阶段失败，阻断唯一允许的外部审查路线。
 
 ### 影响版本与环境
 
 | 项目 | 已观测值 |
 |---|---|
 | 操作系统 | Windows |
 | App host chain | `pwsh.exe -> codex.exe -> ChatGPT.exe -> explorer.exe` |
 | 用户 / profile | `grok login --oauth`、`grok models` 与 Hopper 均在同一用户、同一 profile 语境中执行 |
 | Hopper | `hopper-dispatch` 0.49.1 |
 | Grok CLI | 0.2.118 |
 | Grok binary | 唯一发现的 binary：`C:\Users\litianyi\.grok\bin\grok.exe` |
 | 请求的 vendor / model / reasoning / sandbox | `grok` / `grok-4.5` / `high` / `read-only` |
 | Windows sandbox 强制性 | `unknown/unverified`；本文不把请求的 `read-only` 误报为已独立验证的实际隔离 |
 
 ### 背景与期望
 
 用户已执行 `grok login --oauth`。在上述同一 App host chain 内：
 
 1. `grok models` 以 exit 0 完成。
 2. `hopper-dispatch --setup grok --deep` 显示 `READY`；live catalog 为 1/1，模型为
    `grok-4.5`，并且它报告的是同一 Grok binary。
 3. OAuth artifact 仅确认**存在**；本记录不读取、复制或输出其内容。
 
 因此，至少应当能够区分「model catalog 可访问」与「真实 prompt 执行的认证 /
 授权 / 协议问题」。如果真实派发失败，诊断应当准确、脱敏且可归因，而不能只让
 已通过 catalog 探测的环境得到无可行动依据的 `adapter-auth-failed`。
 
 补充观察：`HOME` 与 `GROK_HOME` 为空时，CLI 会给出
 `auto worktree gc failed...` warning；但 `grok models` 仍以 exit 0 完成。该 warning
 是待检验的影响因素，不是已确认根因。
 
 ### 实际结果
 
 本次新建的独立任务为
 `T-HAWK-VENDOR-POLICY-GROK-ONLY-REVIEW-AUTHED-20260805`。它先 `resolve` 成功，随后以
 下列精确命令派发：
 
 ```text
 hopper-dispatch T-HAWK-VENDOR-POLICY-GROK-ONLY-REVIEW-AUTHED-20260805 --background --vendor grok --model grok-4.5 --reasoning high --sandbox read-only
 ```

 <a id="stale-status-on-runner-death"></a>
 
 ## stale-status-on-runner-death
 
 ---
 
 ## Issue: runner 异常终止后，任务状态文件停在陈旧值（工作实际已完成）
 
 **发现**：test-harnessloop，2026-08-10，任务 T-088（codex，`code-review-adversarial`）
 **严重度**：中——不丢数据，但会让调用方**误判任务未完成**，进而重复派发、浪费 vendor 调用
 
 ### 现象
 
 `--progress` 与 `T-088-output.md` 报告：
 
 ```
 status: in-progress
 phase:  starting
 pid:    71484
 ```
 
 而实际情况：
 
 - `ps -p 71484` **无输出**——runner 进程早已不存在
 - `T-088-output.log` 最后修改 **01:29:11**，查看时为 **10:32**，静止 9 小时
 - **该 log 里有完整产出**：四问裁决、`## Verdict`、`## Next recommendation` 一应俱全
 
 即：**vendor 侧工作已完成、产出已落盘，但状态文件从未被更新到终态**，`phase` 甚至停在最初的 `starting`。
 
 ### 影响
 
 1. 调用方按状态字段判断会得出「仍在跑」，从而**无限等待**或**重复派发**（本次即差点重派第三次，每次都是真实 vendor 花费）。
 2. `--watch` / `--progress` 这类基于状态文件的接口在此场景下**给出错误答案**。
 3. 与本项目既有纪律「不得仅凭 exit 0 / vendor 自述 success 采信」形成对称问题：**同样不能仅凭状态字段判定任务未完成**。本次是靠人工去读 raw log 才发现的。
 
 ### 复现线索（未逐条验证，供排查方向）
 
 同一任务先前有一次 `adapter-timeout` 失败（`duration_ms: 495138`，基线上限 300s），随后以 `--timeout 1500000` 重派。**怀疑与「重派同一 task-id 时状态文件的接管/覆盖」或「runner 被信号终止时缺少 finalize」有关**——两者都会导致终态写入被跳过。
 
 ### 建议方向（不预设实现）
 
 - runner 退出路径（含被信号杀死）加 finalize，至少把 `status` 落成 `failed`/`unknown`，而不是留在 `in-progress`
 - `--progress` / `--watch` 在读到 `status: in-progress` 时，**交叉核对 `pid` 是否存活**；进程不存在则报「状态陈旧」而非「仍在运行」
 - 若 raw log 已含终态标记（如 `## Verdict`），可据此提示「产出可能已完整，请核对 raw log」
 
 ### 与既有 issue 的区别（已核对，非重复）
 
 `ISSUE-progress-watch-hang.md`（2026-06-17）讲的是**测试文件本身挂起**——`tests/unit/progress-watch.test.js` 让 `npm test` 不终止。
 本 issue 讲的是**运行时状态文件在 runner 死后不落终态**，两者根因与影响面不同，仅同属 progress/watch 面。
 
 ### 归档说明
 
 本文件按 hopper 仓既有惯例（`ISSUE-*.md`）落在仓库根，与 `ISSUE-codex-review-hijack.md`、`ISSUE-resolve-ignores-vendor-override.md` 等并列。**未修**，仅登记。
 
 ---

<a id="prompt-artifact-lifecycle-and-windows-permissions"></a>

## prompt-artifact-lifecycle-and-windows-permissions

> Archived from `ISSUE-prompt-artifact-lifecycle-and-windows-permissions.md`. Body verbatim.

## ISSUE: `-prompt.md` artifacts hold the full task brief, accumulate forever, and are unprotected on Windows

- **Status**: open — recorded, not fixed
- **Found**: 2026-08-03, while fixing a Windows CI failure that was itself unrelated
- **Severity**: not a credential leak (this repo's brief discipline forbids real
  credentials in briefs), but a file containing the complete composed prompt sits
  on disk indefinitely, with permissions that are unverified on Windows.

### What the file is

`cli/src/prompt-delivery.js`'s `resolvePromptDelivery()` writes
`<hopperDir>/handoffs/<taskId>-prompt.md` — **inside the project**, not a system
temp dir — with a best-effort `chmodSync(0600)`. It contains the **entire
composed prompt**: execution-mode guardrail + governance constitution and
per-vendor overlay (when `.hopper/GOVERNANCE.md` exists) + the task-type frame +
the task spec. That is everything the vendor would otherwise receive on argv or
stdin, not a fragment.

It is written from two call sites: the sync path (`cli/src/dispatch.js:533`) and
the background path (`cli/bin/hopper-dispatch:1148`).

**This is a necessary mechanism, not a stray artifact.** `cli/src/archive.js:26`
already lists `-prompt.md` in `ARTIFACT_SUFFIXES` alongside `-output.md`,
`-output.log`, `-output-raw.txt` and `-progress.log` as one of the five canonical
per-task artifacts, and the background path documents that the runner pipes this
file into the vendor's stdin precisely because the dispatcher process has already
exited. Deleting it eagerly would break that.

### Two problems

#### 1. It fires far more often on Windows than the size gate suggests

Two independent gates trigger pointer-file delivery:

- **size-gated**: the inline command line exceeds the OS-regime budget
  (cmd-shim 4000B / native-exe 28000B / posix 100000B).
- **newline-gated**: on Windows, when the vendor is reached through a
  `.cmd`/`.bat` cmd.exe shim **and** the adapter cannot read stdin
  (`adapter.promptStdin !== 'supported'`) **and** the prompt contains any
  newline — **regardless of size**.

`composePrompt()` (`cli/src/tasks.js:143-154`) always joins sections with
`'\n\n---\n\n'`, so every real prompt is multi-line. Kimi
(`cli/src/vendors/kimi.js:32`, `stdinMode: 'none'`) is exactly the non-stdin
case. Net effect: on Windows, **essentially every dispatch to a non-stdin vendor
writes one of these**, not just oversized ones.

#### 2. Nothing ever deletes them

`archive.js`'s own header states archival is "EXPLICIT — hopper has no reaction
core, so nothing auto-archives," and `--archive` only **moves** files into
`.hopper/archive/<date>/`. There is no retention window, no purge, no delete path
anywhere for any of the five artifact types. A `-prompt.md` persists until a
human removes it out of band. Combined with (1), Windows accumulates them fastest.

#### 3. The `0600` is best-effort, and on Windows we now know it does not hold

The code comments the `chmodSync` as best-effort because NTFS ACLs do not map to
POSIX mode bits. Independently confirmed the same day (see
`CHANGELOG.md` 0.45.0 and the `windows-latest` diagnostic): hopper's own
owner-only hardening does not actually take effect on Windows —
`icacls /inheritance:r /grant:r` leaves explicit `NT AUTHORITY\SYSTEM` and
`BUILTIN\Administrators` full-control ACEs in place. There is no reason to expect
`chmodSync` to do better. So on Windows these files are, in practice, readable by
every administrator on the machine — which for a single-user dev box is close to
"no protection at all."

### Why it was invisible

The same reason as everything else surfaced in this batch: **nothing executed
it.** The repo had no CI, so the Windows delivery path had never run in a test
environment, and no test asserts anything about artifact retention on any
platform.

### Not claimed

- No evidence any real credential has ever been written to one of these files;
  this repo's `.hopper/AGENTS.md` discipline is that briefs carry parameter names,
  never values. The concern is task context and governance text, not secrets.
- No fix direction is endorsed here. Eager deletion is **wrong** (the background
  runner reads the file after the dispatcher exits). A retention window, an
  explicit purge command, or moving delivery to a mode-enforced temp location are
  all plausible; none has been evaluated.

    28	export function parseQueueContent(content) {
    29	  const lines = content.split(/\r?\n/);
    30	  const rows = [];
    31	
    32	  // Find the table — locate header row containing "Task-type" column
    33	  let inTable = false;
    34	  let columnMap = null;
    35	  let pastSeparator = false;
    36	
    37	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    38	    const rawLine = lines[i];
    39	    const lineNo = i + 1;
    40	    const line = rawLine.trim();
    41	    if (!line.startsWith('|')) {
    42	      // Exiting table or pre-table content
    43	      if (inTable && pastSeparator) {
    44	        inTable = false;
    45	        columnMap = null;
    46	        pastSeparator = false;
    47	      }
    48	      continue;
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    // Inside a markdown table row
    52	    const cells = parseRowCells(line);
    53	
    54	    if (columnMap == null) {
    55	      // First | row = column headers
    56	      columnMap = mapColumns(cells);
    57	      if (columnMap.taskTypeIdx == null && columnMap.roleIdx == null) {
    58	        // Not a queue table; reset
    59	        columnMap = null;
    60	        continue;
    61	      }
    62	      // Number of columns the header declares. Every data row below is
    63	      // required to split into exactly this many cells — see the
    64	      // cell-count guard below for why (E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH).
    65	      columnMap.cellCount = cells.length;
    66	      inTable = true;
    67	      continue;
    68	    }
    69	
    70	    if (!pastSeparator) {
    71	      // Should be the |---|---|... separator row
    72	      if (cells.every((c) => /^:?-+:?$/.test(c.trim()))) {
    73	        pastSeparator = true;
    74	      }
    75	      continue;
    76	    }
    77	
    78	    // Data row.
    79	    //
    80	    // Fail-closed cell-count guard (queue-brief-truncated-by-unescaped-pipe,
    81	    // docs/archive/ISSUES.md): extractRow() below reads cells purely BY INDEX
    82	    // (cells[map.briefIdx], cells[map.vendorIdx], ...). A literal '|' inside
    83	    // a cell — most commonly the Brief — used to split that row into extra
    84	    // cells and silently shift every column after it: the tail of the Brief
    85	    // (and whatever came after it) got dropped with no error, and dispatch
    86	    // proceeded on the truncated text. Refuse to index into a row whose cell
    87	    // count doesn't match the header's declared column count instead of
    88	    // guessing which cells are "real".
    89	    if (cells.length !== columnMap.cellCount) {
    90	      const err = new Error(
    91	        `${E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH}: queue.md row at line ${lineNo} has ${cells.length} ` +
    92	        `cell(s) but the header declares ${columnMap.cellCount} column(s): ${line}\n` +
    93	        `A literal '|' inside a cell (e.g. Brief) must be escaped as \\| — parseRowCells() ` +
    94	        `honors \\| as a literal pipe character, not a column separator. Escape it and re-run; ` +
    95	        `refusing to guess which cells are which.`
    96	      );
    97	      err.code = E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH;
    98	      throw err;
    99	    }
   100	
   101	    const row = extractRow(cells, columnMap);
   102	    if (row) rows.push(row);
   103	  }
   104	
   105	  return rows;
   106	}
   107	
   108	/** Error code: a queue.md data row's cell count doesn't match the header's declared column count. */
   109	export const E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH = 'E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH';
   110	
   111	function parseRowCells(line) {
   112	  // Remove leading and trailing pipes, then split on '|', except a
   113	  // backslash-escaped pipe ('\|') which is honored as a literal '|'
   114	  // character and does NOT start a new cell (see E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH
   115	  // above — this is the half of the fix that makes the guard's own error
   116	  // message true: it tells the caller to escape with \|, so this parser
   117	  // must actually treat \| as a literal pipe rather than lying about it).
   118	  // Markdown table syntax: | a | b | c |
   119	  const trimmed = line.replace(/^\|/, '').replace(/\|\s*$/, '');
   120	  const cells = [];
   121	  let current = '';
   122	  for (let i = 0; i < trimmed.length; i++) {
   123	    const ch = trimmed[i];
   124	    if (ch === '\\' && trimmed[i + 1] === '|') {
   125	      current += '|';
   126	      i++; // consume the escaped pipe too
   127	      continue;
   128	    }
   129	    if (ch === '|') {
   130	      cells.push(current.trim());
   131	      current = '';
   132	      continue;
   133	    }
   134	    current += ch;
   135	  }
   136	  cells.push(current.trim());
   137	  return cells;
   138	}
   139	
   140	function mapColumns(headerCells) {
   141	  const lower = headerCells.map((c) => c.toLowerCase());
   142	  return {
   143	    idIdx: indexOfAny(lower, ['id', 'task id', 'task-id']),
   144	    taskTypeIdx: indexOfAny(lower, ['task-type', 'task_type', 'tasktype', 'type']),
   145	    roleIdx: indexOfAny(lower, ['role']),
   146	    statusIdx: indexOfAny(lower, ['status']),
   147	    dependsIdx: indexOfAny(lower, ['depends', 'dependencies', 'deps']),
   148	    priorityIdx: indexOfAny(lower, ['priority']),
   149	    briefIdx: indexOfAny(lower, ['brief', 'summary', 'description']),
   150	    vendorIdx: indexOfAny(lower, ['vendor']),
   151	    governIdx: indexOfAny(lower, ['govern', 'governance']),
   152	  };
   153	}
   154	
   155	function indexOfAny(arr, candidates) {
   155	function indexOfAny(arr, candidates) {
   156	  for (const cand of candidates) {
   157	    const idx = arr.indexOf(cand);
   158	    if (idx !== -1) return idx;
   159	  }
   160	  return null;
   161	}
   162	
   163	function extractRow(cells, map) {
   164	  const id = map.idIdx != null ? stripBackticks(cells[map.idIdx]) : null;
   165	  if (!id) return null;
   166	
   167	  const taskType = map.taskTypeIdx != null ? stripBackticks(cells[map.taskTypeIdx]) : null;
   168	  const role = map.roleIdx != null ? stripBackticks(cells[map.roleIdx]) : null;
   169	
   170	  // Per USAGE-GUIDE §3.4: Task-type is the primary routing key.
   171	  // Role is decorative (legacy). If both present, Task-type wins.
   172	  const effectiveType = taskType || role || 'unknown';
   173	
   174	  // Per codex final strict audit P1 (Category A): previously unknown statuses
   175	  // silently mapped to 'pending', which re-eligibilizes failed tasks. We now
   176	  // preserve unknown status verbatim and surface it as 'unknown' for the
   177	  // caller; findEligibleTask only treats 'pending' as eligible. A row with
   178	  // illegal status will fail eligibility check rather than silently re-run.
   179	  const rawStatus = map.statusIdx != null ? cells[map.statusIdx].toLowerCase() : 'pending';
   180	  const validStatuses = ['pending', 'in-progress', 'done', 'failed', 'removed'];
   181	  const finalStatus = validStatuses.includes(rawStatus) ? rawStatus : `unknown:${rawStatus}`;
   182	
   183	  const dependsRaw = map.dependsIdx != null ? cells[map.dependsIdx] : '';
   184	  const depends = dependsRaw
   185	    .split(',')
   186	    .map((d) => stripBackticks(d.trim()))
   187	    .filter(Boolean);
   188	
   189	  const priorityRaw = map.priorityIdx != null ? cells[map.priorityIdx].toLowerCase() : 'normal';
   190	  const validPriorities = ['high', 'normal', 'low'];
   191	  const priority = validPriorities.includes(priorityRaw) ? priorityRaw : 'normal';
   192	
   193	  const brief = map.briefIdx != null ? cells[map.briefIdx] : '';
   194	  const vendor = map.vendorIdx != null && cells[map.vendorIdx] ? stripBackticks(cells[map.vendorIdx]) : null;
   195	  const govern = map.governIdx != null && cells[map.governIdx] ? stripBackticks(cells[map.governIdx]) : null;
   196	
   197	  return { id, taskType: effectiveType, status: finalStatus, depends, priority, brief, vendor, govern };
   198	}
   199	
   200	function stripBackticks(s) {
   201	  if (!s) return s;
   202	  return s.replace(/^`/, '').replace(/`$/, '').trim();
   203	}
   204	
   205	/**
   206	 * Find a task by ID; validate it's eligible to dispatch (pending + deps done).
   207	 *
   208	 * @param {import('./types.js').TaskRow[]} tasks
   209	 * @param {string} taskId
   210	 * @returns {{ task: import('./types.js').TaskRow | null, reason: string|null }}
   211	 */
   212	export function findEligibleTask(tasks, taskId) {
   213	  const task = tasks.find((t) => t.id === taskId);
   214	  if (!task) {
   215	    return { task: null, reason: `task ${taskId} not found in queue.md` };
   216	  }
   217	  if (task.status !== 'pending') {
   218	    return { task: null, reason: `task ${taskId} status is '${task.status}', expected 'pending'` };
   219	  }
   220	  for (const depId of task.depends) {
   221	    const dep = tasks.find((t) => t.id === depId);
   222	    if (!dep) {
   223	      return { task: null, reason: `dependency ${depId} not found in queue.md` };
   224	    }
   225	    if (dep.status !== 'done') {
   226	      return { task: null, reason: `dependency ${depId} status is '${dep.status}', expected 'done'` };
   227	    }
   228	  }
   229	  return { task, reason: null };
   230	}
   231	
   232	/**
   233	 * Summarize queue by status (for --status command).
   234	 *
   235	 * @param {import('./types.js').TaskRow[]} tasks
   236	 */
   237	export function summarizeQueue(tasks) {
   238	  const counts = { pending: 0, 'in-progress': 0, done: 0, failed: 0, removed: 0 };
   239	  for (const t of tasks) {
   240	    if (counts[t.status] != null) counts[t.status]++;
    20	tweak.

    22	## [0.56.0] - 2026-08-12
    23	
    24	### Fixed — `queue.md` 的 Brief 若含未转义 `|`，会被静默截断且悄悄改派给别的 vendor
    25	
    26	`cli/src/queue.js` 的行解析按**下标**取值——`extractRow()`（`:163`）里的
    27	`cells[map.briefIdx]`（`:193`）、`cells[map.vendorIdx]`（`:194`）——而列切分
    28	（原 `parseRowCells()`，naive `trimmed.split('|')`）**从不校验切出来的 cell 数是否等于表头
    29	声明的列数**。表头 7 列时，Brief 单元格里只要含一个字面量 `|`，这一行就会被切出 8 个及以上
    30	cell：Brief 被腰斩，之后每一列全体右移一位。经验证（详见
    31	`docs/archive/ISSUES.md#queue-brief-truncated-by-unescaped-pipe`）：
    32	
    33	```
    34	brief 段：前半段任务 | codex | 后半段被吃掉的关键要求
    35	解析出：brief="前半段任务"  vendor="codex"  —— 无任何报错
    36	```
    37	
    38	`.hopper/AGENTS.md` 的 Approved-Vendors 白名单**不是**这个问题的防线——它拦的是"vendor 名不
    39	认识"，只有当被腰斩后紧跟的 token 恰好不是已批准 vendor 名时才会碰巧报错；一旦恰好是
    40	`codex` 这类已批准名字，就完全静默放行，vendor 收到一份被截断的任务书还照常派发。
    41	
    42	**修法：cell 数校验，fail-closed。** `parseQueueContent()`（`cli/src/queue.js:28`）在解析每个
    43	数据行时，先记下表头声明的列数（`columnMap.cellCount`，`:65`），逐行比对；数量不等就**拒绝
    44	整行**、抛出 `E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH`（`:89-99`），不再按下标猜哪个 cell 是哪个字段——多切
    45	（Brief 含裸 `|`）与少切（漏写一列）两种畸形行都会被拦。
    46	
    47	**没有发一条自己不兑现的消息。** 这类缺陷的家族特征是"提示的补救办法解析器根本不支持"——如果
    48	只是把错误文案改成"请把 `|` 转义成 `\|`"，却不让解析器真的认识 `\|`，那只是换了个地方继续
    49	说谎。选择的是**真的实现转义**（而不是"闭口不提转义、只说明允许什么"这条备选）：`\|` 现在
    50	在 `parseRowCells()`（`:111-138`）里被当作字面量 `|` 处理、不再触发分列，未转义的裸 `|`
    51	仍然分列。选这条路是因为本仓自己的 queue.md 里已经出现过在 brief 正文中引用竖线表格行/管道
    52	语法的真实需求（如 `.hopper/handoffs/leader-tasklist.md` 与本仓 T-102 系列 brief），完全禁止
    53	字面量 `|` 会挡住这类合法内容；转义之后，round-trip 结果是**完整**文本（含那个 `|` 字符），
    54	不再是被腰斩的前半段。
    55	
    56	**对已存量 `.hopper/queue.md` 的影响是真实的，不是假想。** 这条守卫是回溯性的——旧文件里任何
    57	一行的 cell 数只要与表头不符（无论是不是因为未转义的 `|`，比如一行只有裸 `| |` 这种残留的
    58	空白间隔行）都会让 `parseQueue()`/`parseQueueContent()` 直接抛错、拒绝整个文件，而不是像修复前
    59	那样把畸形行悄悄跳过（`extractRow()` 对空 `id` 返回 `null`）后继续解析其余行。这是有意的：让
    60	一份格式有问题的 queue.md 保持"看起来能用"，正是这个缺陷家族的根本形状。已知会中招的行必须在
    61	各自项目的 `.hopper/queue.md` 里修掉，这个包本身不做任何放宽。
    62	
    63	**测试**：`tests/unit/queue.test.js` 新增 5 条——原始缺陷复现（8 cell vs 7 column，必须抛
    64	`E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH` 而不是产出 `brief="前半段任务" vendor="codex"`）、错误文案必须
    65	提及它实际支持的 `\|`、无竖线的正常行原样通过（不过度拒绝）、`\|` 转义后完整往返（含那个
    66	`|` 字符）、cell 数**偏少**（漏写一列）同样被拒。四条依赖新校验的测试逐一用破坏性反证验证：
    67	临时禁用 cell 数校验后 3 条转红（另 1 条不依赖校验、本就不受影响），恢复后全绿。
    68	
    69	版本升 minor：修的是用户可观察的行为（一个此前完全静默的截断+误派现在会被拒绝并报错），
    70	按本文件「Versioning」一节的约定不算 patch 级微调。
    71	
    72	## [0.55.1] - 2026-08-12
    73	
    74	### Fixed — `package-lock.json` 的版本字段落后 5 个发布，而它明明写在发布清单里
    75	
    76	`package-lock.json` 的 `version` 停在 0.50.0，仓库已经发到 0.55.0——落后五个 release 无人
    77	察觉。但这次不是清单漏写：项目自己的发布清单明确列出了 `package-lock.json` 是需要跟着
    78	一起 bump 的位置之一。问题不在清单不完整，在**一份写下来的清单不是机制**——没人拿它当程序
    79	跑，遗漏就只能靠人记得。

   172	// --- queue-brief-truncated-by-unescaped-pipe (docs/archive/ISSUES.md) ---
   173	//
   174	// cells[map.briefIdx] / cells[map.vendorIdx] used to be read purely by index,
   175	// with no check that a row actually split into as many cells as the header
   176	// declares. A literal '|' inside the Brief cell shifted every column after
   177	// it, silently truncating the brief and misassigning Vendor — with NO error,
   178	// because the shifted-in token often happened to be an approved vendor name.
   179	// These tests pin the fail-closed cell-count guard that replaces that
   180	// silent-indexing behavior.
   181	
   182	const QUEUE_HEADER = '| ID | Task-type | Status | Depends | Priority | Brief | Vendor |';
   183	const QUEUE_SEP = '|----|-----------|--------|---------|----------|-------|--------|';
   184	
   185	test('parseQueueContent rejects (fail-closed) a row whose unescaped Brief pipe produces extra cells, instead of silently truncating the brief and shifting Vendor', () => {
   186	  // Exact repro from the issue: brief="前半段任务" vendor="codex" used to come
   187	  // out with NO error and the tail silently dropped. It must now throw.
   188	  const badRow = '| T-C | code-review-adversarial | pending | | high | 前半段任务 | codex | 后半段被吃掉的关键要求 |';
   189	  const content = `${QUEUE_HEADER}\n${QUEUE_SEP}\n${badRow}\n`;
   190	
   191	  let caught = null;
   192	  let rows = null;
   193	  try {
   194	    rows = parseQueueContent(content);
   195	  } catch (err) {
   196	    caught = err;
   197	  }
   198	
   199	  assert.equal(rows, null, 'must not return a parsed rows array for a mismatched row');
   200	  assert.ok(caught, 'must throw instead of silently indexing into the shifted row');
   201	  assert.equal(caught.code, E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH);
   202	  assert.match(caught.message, /8 cell\(s\)/, 'error must state the actual (wrong) cell count');
   203	  assert.match(caught.message, /7 column\(s\)/, 'error must state the header-declared column count');
   204	  // Guard against the exact old silent-truncation symptom re-appearing under
   205	  // a different code path: the old (defective) behavior produced this pair.
   206	  assert.notDeepEqual(
   207	    rows && rows[0] && { brief: rows[0].brief, vendor: rows[0].vendor },
   208	    { brief: '前半段任务', vendor: 'codex' }
   209	  );
   210	});
   211	
   212	test('parseQueueContent error message does not instruct an escape mechanism the parser fails to honor', () => {
   213	  // Design constraint: if the error tells the user to escape '|' as '\|',
   214	  // parseRowCells() must actually treat '\|' as literal — otherwise this is
   215	  // exactly the "lying message" failure family the issue belongs to.
   216	  const badRow = '| T-C | code-impl | pending | | high | a | b | c |';
   217	  const content = `${QUEUE_HEADER}\n${QUEUE_SEP}\n${badRow}\n`;
   218	  assert.throws(() => parseQueueContent(content), (err) => {
   219	    assert.match(err.message, /\\\|/, 'message must mention the \\| escape it claims to support');
   220	    return true;
   221	  });
   222	  // The other half of the constraint (that \| really is honored) is proven
   223	  // by the round-trip test below.
   224	});
   225	
   226	test('parseQueueContent still parses a normal row with no pipes in the Brief identically to before (no over-rejection)', () => {
   227	  const goodRow = '| T-A | code-impl | pending | | normal | plain brief text, no literal pipes here | codex |';
   228	  const content = `${QUEUE_HEADER}\n${QUEUE_SEP}\n${goodRow}\n`;
   229	  const rows = parseQueueContent(content);
   230	  assert.equal(rows.length, 1);
   231	  assert.equal(rows[0].id, 'T-A');
   232	  assert.equal(rows[0].brief, 'plain brief text, no literal pipes here');
   233	  assert.equal(rows[0].vendor, 'codex');
   234	});
   235	
   236	test('parseQueueContent unescapes \\| to a literal pipe inside a cell, round-tripping the FULL text (including what used to be truncated)', () => {
   237	  const escRow = '| T-B | code-impl | pending | | normal | 前半段任务 \\| 后半段仍完整保留的关键要求 | codex |';
   238	  const content = `${QUEUE_HEADER}\n${QUEUE_SEP}\n${escRow}\n`;
   239	  const rows = parseQueueContent(content);
   240	  assert.equal(rows.length, 1);
   241	  assert.equal(rows[0].brief, '前半段任务 | 后半段仍完整保留的关键要求');
   242	  assert.equal(rows[0].vendor, 'codex');
   243	});
   244	
   245	test('parseQueueContent rejects a row with FEWER cells than the header declares (missing column, not just an extra one)', () => {
   246	  // Six cells where the header declares seven (Depends column dropped) — the
   247	  // same guard must also catch under-count, not just over-count from a pipe.
   248	  const shortRow = '| T-C | code-impl | pending | normal | short row missing a column | codex |';
   249	  const content = `${QUEUE_HEADER}\n${QUEUE_SEP}\n${shortRow}\n`;
   250	  assert.throws(() => parseQueueContent(content), (err) => {
   251	    assert.equal(err.code, E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH);
   252	    assert.match(err.message, /6 cell\(s\)/);
   253	    assert.match(err.message, /7 column\(s\)/);
   254	    return true;
   255	  });

codex
## Summary

0.56.0 的指定三类解析用例成立，`\|` 也确实可以完整往返；但当前实现仍有等宽抵消绕过，并使仓库自己的真实 `queue.md` 无法解析。真实集成测试出现 4 个产品失败，ISSUES 状态索引也不正确，因此当前版本不具备发布条件。

## Files touched

none — 严格只读评审，未创建、修改或删除文件。

## Acceptance verification (6/6 reviewed)

### Q1 — PARTIAL：指定修复成立，但不能完全消除静默截断

直接用管道向真实 `parseQueue('/dev/stdin')` 提供三类输入，检查结构化结果而非 exit code：

```text
未转义：
{"ok":false,"code":"E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH",..."8 cell(s)"..."7 column(s)"}

正常：
{"ok":true,"rows":[{"brief":"完整正常任务书","vendor":"codex",...}]}

转义：
{"ok":true,"rows":[{"brief":"前半段任务 | 后半段要求","vendor":"codex",...}]}
```

`node --test tests/unit/queue.test.js`：19/19 通过。

但列数守卫存在“等宽抵消”绕过。本仓既有行允许省略末尾 Vendor，原本只有 6 cells；Brief 中恰好增加一个裸 `| codex` 后总数变成 7，守卫通过：

```text
输入意图：Brief="完整意图前半段 | codex"，Vendor 省略
实际：{"brief":"完整意图前半段","vendor":"codex"}
findEligibleTask：reason=null
```

根因是 [queue.js](/Users/litianyi/Documents/Code/_ai-goods/test-harnessloop/hopper-plugin/cli/src/queue.js:89) 只校验总数相等，随后仍按下标读取。

### Q2 — FAIL：扫描器本身无存量转义冲突，但整体改动破坏真实合法队列

`rg -F '\|' .hopper/queue.md .hopper/handoffs/leader-tasklist.md` 无匹配，因此扫描器没有重新解释现存 `\|`。`leader-tasklist.md` 也不经过 `parseRowCells`，其语义不受该扫描器影响。

但是直接执行：

```text
parseQueue(".hopper/queue.md")
→ E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH at line 19: 6 cells vs 7 columns
```

[queue.md 第 19–36 行](/Users/litianyi/Documents/Code/_ai-goods/test-harnessloop/hopper-plugin/.hopper/queue.md:19) 共 18 行省略了末尾可选 Vendor cell；旧解析器会将 Vendor 置为 `null`，而新守卫让整个文件失败。[真实夹具测试](/Users/litianyi/Documents/Code/_ai-goods/test-harnessloop/hopper-plugin/tests/integration/real-fixtures.test.js:22) 也明确要求该队列可解析。

### Q3 — REWORK：内容行应整文件 fail-closed，纯空占位行不应如此

实测：

```text
短纯空占位行 "| |"
→ E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH: 1 vs 7

完整宽度纯空行 "| | | | | | | |"
→ 被 extractRow 正常忽略，后续合法任务成功解析
```

结论：

- 有任何实质内容、但列数不符的行，应让整个文件失败。忽略它可能改变依赖图、状态或目标任务，整文件 fail-closed 是正确粒度。
- 所有 cells 均为空的纯占位行应在列数守卫前跳过。它没有 ID、状态或任务内容，旧行为本来也会忽略；仅因空格行宽度不同而阻断整个控制面过狠。
- 不建议普遍给短内容行自动补空 cell，因为无法可靠区分“遗漏末尾 Vendor”和“遗漏中间 Depends”。应迁移真实队列，为空 Vendor 写出显式 `| |`，并把精确等宽要求写入格式规范。

### Q4 — PASS_WITH_NOTE：`\|` 指示真实可用

转义输入通过真实 `parseQueue` 得到完整 Brief：

```text
"前半段任务 \| 后半段要求"
→ "前半段任务 | 后半段要求"
```

因此错误文案没有虚构转义能力。不过同一文案也用于少 cell 和纯空占位行；这些情形没有需要转义的裸 pipe，建议按 over-count/under-count 分别提示“转义 pipe”或“补齐缺失 cell”。

### Q5 — FAIL：部分发布守卫通过，但真实夹具、ISSUES 与 CHANGELOG 未就绪

通过项：

- `node --test tests/unit/version-discovery.test.js`：3/3 通过。
- `node cli/bin/hopper-dispatch --version`：`0.56.0`。
- `node --test tests/unit/vendored-plugin-sync.test.js`：3/3 通过。
- 主、副本 `queue.js` 逐字一致。
- `cli/src/tasks.js` 工作区与 HEAD hash 均为 `644ac62ab347674ce5bf5bb9cedee64976b2d13e`，一字未改。
- `git diff --check` 通过。

失败项：

- `node --test tests/integration/real-fixtures.test.js`：3 pass / 4 fail；四项均因真实 `queue.md` 第 19 行 `6 vs 7`，属于产品失败。
- [ISSUES 索引](/Users/litianyi/Documents/Code/_ai-goods/test-harnessloop/hopper-plugin/docs/archive/ISSUES.md:21) 写 Open 9 / Closed 13，但 `prompt-artifact-lifecycle-and-windows-permissions` 被放在 Closed，其[正文状态仍是 open](/Users/litianyi/Documents/Code/_ai-goods/test-harnessloop/hopper-plugin/docs/archive/ISSUES.md:1743)。正确分类应为 Open 10 / Closed 12。
- [CHANGELOG](/Users/litianyi/Documents/Code/_ai-goods/test-harnessloop/hopper-plugin/CHANGELOG.md:29) 声称一个裸 pipe 会产生“8 个及以上 cells”，但省略末尾 Vendor 的既有 6-cell 行只会变成 7，恰好绕过守卫；“无过度拒绝”测试也未覆盖仓库真实夹具。

只读环境导致以下测试均在断言前因 `mkdtempSync` 返回 EPERM，未计作产品失败：

- `tests/unit/dispatch-task-content.test.js`：12/12 环境失败。

## Summary

0.56.0 的指定三类解析用例成立，`\|` 也确实可以完整往返；但当前实现仍有等宽抵消绕过，并使仓库自己的真实 `queue.md` 无法解析。真实集成测试出现 4 个产品失败，ISSUES 状态索引也不正确，因此当前版本不具备发布条件。

none — 严格只读评审，未创建、修改或删除文件。

## Acceptance verification (6/6 reviewed)

### Q1 — PARTIAL：指定修复成立，但不能完全消除静默截断

直接用管道向真实 `parseQueue('/dev/stdin')` 提供三类输入，检查结构化结果而非 exit code：

```text
未转义：
{"ok":false,"code":"E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH",..."8 cell(s)"..."7 column(s)"}

正常：
{"ok":true,"rows":[{"brief":"完整正常任务书","vendor":"codex",...}]}

转义：
{"ok":true,"rows":[{"brief":"前半段任务 | 后半段要求","vendor":"codex",...}]}
```

`node --test tests/unit/queue.test.js`：19/19 通过。

但列数守卫存在“等宽抵消”绕过。本仓既有行允许省略末尾 Vendor，原本只有 6 cells；Brief 中恰好增加一个裸 `| codex` 后总数变成 7，守卫通过：

```text
输入意图：Brief="完整意图前半段 | codex"，Vendor 省略
实际：{"brief":"完整意图前半段","vendor":"codex"}
findEligibleTask：reason=null
```

根因是 [queue.js](/Users/litianyi/Documents/Code/_ai-goods/test-harnessloop/hopper-plugin/cli/src/queue.js:89) 只校验总数相等，随后仍按下标读取。

### Q2 — FAIL：扫描器本身无存量转义冲突，但整体改动破坏真实合法队列

`rg -F '\|' .hopper/queue.md .hopper/handoffs/leader-tasklist.md` 无匹配，因此扫描器没有重新解释现存 `\|`。`leader-tasklist.md` 也不经过 `parseRowCells`，其语义不受该扫描器影响。

但是直接执行：

```text
parseQueue(".hopper/queue.md")
→ E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH at line 19: 6 cells vs 7 columns
```

[queue.md 第 19–36 行](/Users/litianyi/Documents/Code/_ai-goods/test-harnessloop/hopper-plugin/.hopper/queue.md:19) 共 18 行省略了末尾可选 Vendor cell；旧解析器会将 Vendor 置为 `null`，而新守卫让整个文件失败。[真实夹具测试](/Users/litianyi/Documents/Code/_ai-goods/test-harnessloop/hopper-plugin/tests/integration/real-fixtures.test.js:22) 也明确要求该队列可解析。

### Q3 — REWORK：内容行应整文件 fail-closed，纯空占位行不应如此

实测：

```text
短纯空占位行 "| |"
→ E_ROW_CELL_COUNT_MISMATCH: 1 vs 7

完整宽度纯空行 "| | | | | | | |"
→ 被 extractRow 正常忽略，后续合法任务成功解析
```

结论：

- 有任何实质内容、但列数不符的行，应让整个文件失败。忽略它可能改变依赖图、状态或目标任务，整文件 fail-closed 是正确粒度。
- 所有 cells 均为空的纯占位行应在列数守卫前跳过。它没有 ID、状态或任务内容，旧行为本来也会忽略；仅因空格行宽度不同而阻断整个控制面过狠。
- 不建议普遍给短内容行自动补空 cell，因为无法可靠区分“遗漏末尾 Vendor”和“遗漏中间 Depends”。应迁移真实队列，为空 Vendor 写出显式 `| |`，并把精确等宽要求写入格式规范。

### Q4 — PASS_WITH_NOTE：`\|` 指示真实可用

转义输入通过真实 `parseQueue` 得到完整 Brief：

```text
"前半段任务 \| 后半段要求"
→ "前半段任务 | 后半段要求"
```

因此错误文案没有虚构转义能力。不过同一文案也用于少 cell 和纯空占位行；这些情形没有需要转义的裸 pipe，建议按 over-count/under-count 分别提示“转义 pipe”或“补齐缺失 cell”。

失败项：
